<script>
                    var animationActive = false;
                    $(document).on('click', '.newCurrencyCalcResult', function () {
                        if (animationActive) {
                            return false;
                        }
                        animationActive = true;
                        var $this = $(this);
                        var currentCalcStr = parseFloat($this.attr('data-value')).toString();
                        var dummy = $(this).siblings('.dummyContainer[data-id=' + $(this).attr('data-id') + ']');
                        var top = $(".realCalculator").offset().top - $(this).offset().top;
                        dummy.find('.newCurrencyMainCalcInput').val(1);
                        dummy.css({'display': 'flex'});
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            dummy.css({'top': top + 'px'});

                        }, 200);

                        setTimeout(function () {
                            dummy.css({'display': 'none', 'top': '0px'});
                            $(".realCalculator .newCurrencyMainCalcContainerIcon").html(dummy.find('.newCurrencyMainCalcContainerIcon').html());
                            $(".realCalculator .newCurrencyMainCalcSymbol").html(dummy.find('.newCurrencyMainCalcSymbol').html());
                            $(".realCalculator .newCurrencyMainName").html(dummy.find('.newCurrencyMainName').html());
                            $(".newCurrencyCalcResult").removeClass('selectedResult');
                            $this.addClass('selectedResult');
                            animationActive = false;



                            var thisRate = parseFloat($this.attr('data-acutal-rate'));
                            $.each($(".newCurrencyCalcResult"), function () {
                                var currentRate = parseFloat($(this).attr('data-acutal-rate'));
                                var newRate = currentRate / thisRate;
                                $(this).attr('data-rate', newRate);

                                $(this).attr('title', "<div class='success' style='direction:ltr'>" + $(this).attr('data-code') + " - " + $(this).attr('data-name') + "</div> <div style='direction:ltr'>1 " + $this.attr('data-code') + "  = " + number_format(newRate, 4) + " " + $(this).attr('data-code') + "</div> ");

                            });

                            $(".realCalculator .newCurrencyMainCalcInput").val(1).trigger('keyup');
                            $("[data-toggle=tooltip]").tooltip('fixTitle');

                        }, 1200);

                    });

                    $(document).on('keyup', '.newCurrencyMainCalcInput', function () {
                        var thisVal = parseFloat($(this).val());
                        console.log($(this));
                        $.each($(".newCurrencyCalcResult"), function () {
                            var finalVal = thisVal * parseFloat($(this).attr('data-rate'));
                            console.log(finalVal);
                            $(this).attr('data-value', number_format(finalVal, 4));
                            $(this).find('.newCurrencyCalcResultText').html(number_format(finalVal, 4));

                        });

                    });
                </script>
